# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
Linkedlist
    A linkedlist is a linear data structure, in which elements are stored
    in separate object called nodes. Each node data and a reference(link)
    to another node. ^OfNQWMyO

A linkedlist node contains : 
    - Data : The value stored in the node 
    - Next : A reference to the next node 
   
--> Type of linkedlist: 

1. Single Linkedlist 
    Each node points to the next node only. 
 ^z0KGoXEW

2. Doubly linkedlist 
    Each node points to both the previous and next nodes. 
     ^RkuuWwQU

3. Circular linkedlist 
    The last node points back the first node instead of null.
 ^VBmldjsP

How does linkedlist overcome from the problem of array -
    
    A linkedlist solve some of limitations of array specially when inserting 
or deleting element frequently.

Problem with array  ^ue5gdAXe

Array Before Inserting  ^cW2gMQcK

suppose, we want to insert 25 between 20 & 30. 
 ^LyyTtCx5

we must shift 30 & 40 to the right in a commercial array. If the array
is full, we may also needed to create a larger array and copy its element. ^pYKxKstO

--> A basic Java array has fixed length.
--> Inserting the middle may required shifting element. ^JOrmjfqD

class Node{
    int data;
    Node next;
    
    Node(int data){
        this.data=data;
        this.next=null;
    }
}
class Main{
    public static void main(String arg[])
       {
        Node first = new Node(10);
        Node second = new Node(20);
        Node third = new Node(30);

    //connect the node
    first.next = second;
    second.next = third;
    
    //traverse the linkedlist 
    Node current = first;
    while(current!=null)
        {
    System.out.println(current.data+"->");
    current = current.next;
        }
    System.out.println("null");
    }
        } ^DVhgBnyh

==> Algorithm to insert node in the middle ^zNs5nPtW

Step 1 > Create a new node with the required data.

Step 2 > Start the head node.

Step 3 > Traverse the list until reaching the node after which you 
            want to insert.

Step 4 > Set newNode.next=temp.next

Step 5 > Set temp.next=newNode

Step 6 > Stop ^YzweI5eO

class Node {
    int data;
    Node next;
    Node(int data) {
        this.data = data;
        this.next = null;  }
}
class Main {
    Node head;
    void insertMiddle(int data, int position) {
        Node newNode = new Node(data);
        if (head == null) {
            head = newNode;
            return;
        }
        Node temp = head;
        for (int i = 1; i < position - 1; i++) {
            if (temp.next == null) {
                System.out.println("Invalid position");
                return;
            }
            temp = temp.next;
        }
        newNode.next = temp.next;
        temp.next = newNode;
    }

    void disp() {
        Node temp = head;
        while (temp != null) {
            System.out.print(temp.data + "->");
            temp = temp.next;
        }
        System.out.println("null");
    }
    public static void main(String arg[]) {
        Main list = new Main();
        list.head = new Node(10);
        list.head.next = new Node(20);
        list.head.next.next = new Node(30);
        list.head.next.next.next = new Node(40);

        System.out.println("Before Insertion : ");
        list.disp();

        list.insertMiddle(25, 3);

        System.out.println("After Insertion : ");
        list.disp();
    }
} ^8wxLI1GE
